  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gajah Mada, leader of Indonesia (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Indonesia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Kris Swordsman, Replacing Swordsman (Requires technology: Iron Working)
        - Unique Unit: Djong, Replacing Galleass (Requires technology: Guilds)
        - Unique Building: Candi, Replacing Garden (Requires technology: Theology)
        - Unique Improvement: Kampong (Unique Indonesian Improvement. The Kampong can be built by Embarked Workers on Coastal and Lake Tiles, and not next to another Kampong.
          Each Kampong yields Food, Production, and Culture.); Requires technology: Sailing
        - Civilization Ability: When you gain a City, one of 3 unique Luxuries (//) will appear nearby. +5% to unique Yield and Golden Age duration modifiers from Global Monopolies; +2 to Yields and Happiness from Global Monopolies.
      - Leader: Gajah Mada
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Spirit (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Martial Spirit: +25% Combat Strength when attacking in melee for 50 turns, scaling with game speed. War Weariness reduced by 25%, and Razing Speed is doubled.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - Third Alternative (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Third Alternative: Quantity of Strategic Resources produced (including Paper) is increased by 100%. Reduces Unit Gold Maintenance costs by 25%.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 4
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 5
        - WarBias: 4
        - HostileBias: 5
        - WarmongerHate: 9

        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 7
        - Loyalty: 9
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 2
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 1
        - MinorCivWarBias: 1
        - DenounceWillingness: 4
        - Forgiveness: 5
        - Meanness: 4
        - Neediness: 5
        - Chattiness: 10
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: DIPLOMATIC VICTORY PERSONA MAINTAINED: Critical 1-turn voting window requires maximum delegate protection. VictoryCompetitiveness(10) and MinorCivCompetitiveness(10) essential for tight 3-way race (27 vs 28 vs 28 delegates). High MinorCivFriendlyBias(10) protects Prague, Wittenberg, Winchester alliances providing 6 delegates. Wittenberg Hinduism quest has ONLY 4 turns remaining - must complete before vote. Moderate Boldness(5) and WarBias(4) maintain measured defense against Aztecs/Shoshone while avoiding Iroquois escalation. High Friendliness(9), WorkWithWillingness(9), Chattiness(10) enable diplomatic maneuvering during World Congress voting.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Electronics
        - Rationale: Electronics unlocks Police Stations (+10 City Security, +3 Culture) critical for defending cities against Aztec/Shoshone aggression during World Congress voting. Leads to Telecommunications then Satellites/Apollo Program for late-game diplomatic/science backup options. City security vital when managing multiple active conflicts and protecting delegate sources. Police Stations synergize with Autocracy tenets (New World Order, Police State) for production bonuses. Currently completing Advanced Ballistics (~8 turns), so this sets next research queue position appropriately.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Enlightenment (Policy)
        - Rationale: Enlightenment (Rationalism branch) provides FREE TECHNOLOGY - accelerates victory timeline significantly by immediately advancing toward Satellites/Apollo Program or other critical late-game techs. +1 Happiness from Universities helps maintain stability during wartime with multiple active conflicts. Policy available in ~14 turns, positioning us well for endgame. Rationalism completion supports science output for late-game tech race while pursuing diplomatic victory. Free tech could unlock Satellites faster for Science Victory backup plan if diplomatic route becomes compromised.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Japan
        - Public: -30
        - Private: -40
        - Rationale: Japan has denounced us and we've denounced them. They have Authority branch complete and are at war with Iroquois and Brazil. While not our primary concern during diplomatic victory push, maintaining negative relationship reflects mutual denunciations and divergent interests. Japan has only 2 delegates so they're less critical for World Congress, but their military strength warrants caution. Keeping relationship negative ensures AI maintains appropriate defensive posture.
        - UpdatedTurn: 247
        
        ### Sofia
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Sofia just became our ally (430 influence) and declared war on Aztecs, showing strong alignment with our interests. As a city-state, they provide 2 delegates. Maintaining maximum positive relationship ensures they remain loyal through World Congress voting. Their military action against our enemy demonstrates shared strategic interests.
        - UpdatedTurn: 303
        
        ### The Celts
        - Public: -80
        - Private: -60
        - Rationale: CELTS - WARTIME ENEMY BUT POTENTIAL LIBERATION ALLY: Currently at war with them (100% war score against us), but they are the ONLY potential ally against Iroquois hegemony. Their -80 public reflects active warfare, but -60 private leaves room for post-war reconciliation. Their 627 military strength could be valuable if we can coordinate against Iroquois after World Congress vote. Need to end this war strategically to free up military resources and potentially gain an ally against Iroquois.
        - UpdatedTurn: 411
        
        ### The Iroquois
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: IROQUOIS - EXISTENTIAL THREAT MAXIMIZED: They possess 377k military vs my 6.8k, control 87% of original capitals (7/8), lead World Congress with 28 delegates (1 ahead of my 27), and have atomic bombs positioned near my territories. As my suzerain (capitulated vassal), they pose ultimate threat to sovereignty. Maximum negative relationship ensures AI maintains highest defensive alert, prioritizes nuclear deterrent development, and prepares for potential liberation scenarios post-vote. Critical to secure diplomatic victory before any military confrontation - their air superiority near coastal cities requires maximum defensive posture.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### The Aztecs
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: AZTECS - ACTIVE WAR ENEMY URGENT: They conquered our city of Denpasar, naval forces actively assault our coastal territories. We declared war and captured Tlatelolco. Maximum negative ensures AI prioritizes defending coastal cities (Medan, Makassar, Batam vulnerable) and supports offensive operations to reclaim Denpasar. Their 2,924 military strength is manageable but dangerous with naval units actively attacking. Must secure territory before/during World Congress vote - losing cities during voting could cost us the diplomatic victory in this tight 27 vs 28 vs 28 delegate race.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### The Shoshone
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: SHOSHONE - ACTIVE WAR ENEMY PRIORITY: They declared war on Indonesia with 2,961 military strength, units actively attacking our territory. They denounced Celts (complicating our diplomacy) and follow Freedom ideology (opposing our Autocracy). Maximum negative ensures AI prioritizes defense against Shoshone aggression and supports offensive operations to eliminate this threat before World Congress vote. Their naval bombardment capability threatens coastal cities. Must secure territory to free military resources for Iroquois deterrence and protect city-state allies providing critical delegates.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Prague
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: PRAGUE - CRITICAL CITY-STATE ALLY: At 1031 influence (well above ~200 alliance threshold), they provide 2 delegates for World Congress. Currently at war with Aztecs, Iroquois, Poland, Brazil, and Shoshone - all our enemies or rivals. Their naval units help defend our waters. Technology quest has 1 turn remaining - we lead 10-4, should complete soon. Maximum positive relationship ensures AI prioritizes protecting this alliance. Critical delegate source that must remain loyal through World Congress voting against Brazilian Sphere of Influence proposals. Losing this ally would cost us 2 delegates - potentially decisive in tight 27 vs 28 vs 28 race.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Wittenberg
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: WITTENBERG - URGENT CITY-STATE ALLY: At 656 influence (above alliance threshold), they provide 2 delegates. Hinduism quest has ONLY 4 TURNS REMAINING - this is CRITICAL priority. Must complete before World Congress vote to secure their loyalty. Currently at war with Shoshone and Aztecs (our enemies). Maximum positive relationship ensures AI prioritizes completing their religion quest immediately. Losing this ally to Brazilian Sphere of Influence pressure would cost us 2 delegates - potentially decisive in tight race. Hinduism missionaries may need deployment to Wittenberg urgently.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Winchester
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: WINCHESTER - CRITICAL CITY-STATE ALLY: At 336 influence (above alliance threshold), they provide 2 delegates for World Congress vote. Currently at war with Shoshone and Aztecs (our enemies). Great Artist quest has 43 turns - lower priority but alliance must be preserved. Maximum positive relationship ensures AI prioritizes protecting this alliance. Brazil's sanction proposals and sphere of influence demands threaten city-state independence - must maintain strongest possible bond to prevent losing these critical delegates. Combined with Prague and Wittenberg, these 3 city-states provide 6 delegates - essential for diplomatic victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Poland
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: POLAND - FRIENDLY NEIGHBOR WITH CAUTION: Poland stole our tiles previously but we have Declaration of Friendship and both follow Autocracy. They have only 16 delegates (not a diplomatic threat). Positive relationship reflects potential utility as counterweight to Iroquois/Brazil influence. Their 638 military is weak but territory theft is concerning. Public 40 shows friendship, private 20 reflects underlying caution. May be useful for post-vote diplomatic maneuvering. Both share Autocracy ideology which provides diplomatic bonuses. Currently at war with Shoshone (our enemy) which aligns interests.
        - UpdatedTurn: 423
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -60
        - Rationale: BRAZIL - DIPLOMATIC RIVAL ESCALATED: Brazil has 26 delegates (3 behind my 29), actively pushing Sphere of Influence proposals threatening my city-state alliances. They just LOST Zanzibar to me this turn - they will try to reclaim. Moderate-negative relationship increased to reflect heightened competition during critical voting phase. Brazil has strong economy (8364 gold, +541 GPT) enabling aggressive diplomatic unit purchases. Must ensure AI prioritizes countering Brazilian diplomatic pressure on city-states like Belgrade (444 influence, not yet ally) and protects existing alliances from Brazilian influence campaigns.
        - UpdatedTurn: 424
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: PERSONA MAINTAINED: Current diplomatic victory persona remains optimal. VictoryCompetitiveness(10) and MinorCivCompetitiveness(10) essential for tight 3-way delegate race. MinorCivFriendlyBias(10) protects city-state alliances (Prague 1031, Wittenberg 656, Winchester 336, Zanzibar 674 - all allies providing 8 delegates total). High Friendliness(9), WorkWithWillingness(9), Chattiness(10) enable diplomatic maneuvering during World Congress voting. Moderate Boldness(5) and WarBias(4) maintain measured defense against Aztecs/Shoshone while avoiding Iroquois escalation. Relationships with Iroquois(-100/-100), Aztecs(-100/-100), Shoshone(-100/-100) remain at maximum hostility reflecting active wars and existential threats.

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 41
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 10 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - World Religion: Confucianism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Indonesian Sphere of Influence: Prague:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Global Peace Accords:
            - Description: Unit maintenance costs are raised by 25% Gold. 2.0x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 0.5x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Indonesian Sphere of Influence: Winchester:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Sokoto:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Indonesian Sphere of Influence: Wittenberg:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - EnactedOn: 424
          - World Ideology: Autocracy:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Cape Town:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Raqmu:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Global Hegemony:
            - Description: Designate a player that could achieve Global Hegemony. If a Civilization receives support from enough Delegates in the Congress, that leader will attain Diplomatic Victory. The number of Delegates needed depends on the number of Civilizations and City-States in the game, and can be seen on the World Congress screen.
              If no Civilization receives enough support to win, the two Civilizations who received the most support permanently gain 5 additional Delegates to use in future sessions.
              May not be proposed by a Civilization. Automatically proposed at regular intervals once a World Ideology Resolution is active.
            - Proposer: Unknown

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gajah Mada, leader of Indonesia (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Indonesia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Kris Swordsman, Replacing Swordsman (Requires technology: Iron Working)
        - Unique Unit: Djong, Replacing Galleass (Requires technology: Guilds)
        - Unique Building: Candi, Replacing Garden (Requires technology: Theology)
        - Unique Improvement: Kampong (Unique Indonesian Improvement. The Kampong can be built by Embarked Workers on Coastal and Lake Tiles, and not next to another Kampong.
          Each Kampong yields Food, Production, and Culture.); Requires technology: Sailing
        - Civilization Ability: When you gain a City, one of 3 unique Luxuries (//) will appear nearby. +5% to unique Yield and Golden Age duration modifiers from Global Monopolies; +2 to Yields and Happiness from Global Monopolies.
      - Leader: Gajah Mada
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Spirit (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Martial Spirit: +25% Combat Strength when attacking in melee for 50 turns, scaling with game speed. War Weariness reduced by 25%, and Razing Speed is doubled.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - Third Alternative (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Third Alternative: Quantity of Strategic Resources produced (including Paper) is increased by 100%. Reduces Unit Gold Maintenance costs by 25%.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text:  Let me analyze the current situation carefully.
            
            Current State:
            - Turn 425, playing as Indonesia (Gajah Mada)
            - Victory Progress: Diplomatic Victory is the primary goal
            - World Congress voting in 10 turns
            - Need 41 votes to win diplomatic victory
            - Current delegates: Indonesia 29, Iroquois 33, Brazil 26, Poland 16, Aztecs 10, Shoshone 6
            - Iroquois leads with 33 delegates (80% victory percentage)
            - I'm at 29 delegates (70% victory percentage)
            
            Critical Change from Events:
            - Brazil used a Great Diplomat to take Zanzibar from me (Event 47-49, Turn 424)
            - I then used my own Great Diplomat to reclaim Zanzibar (Events 99-102, Turn 425)
            - So Zanzibar is back as my ally with 667 influence
            
            Active City-State Allies:
            - Prague: 1031 influence (ally) - 2 delegates
            - Wittenberg: 656 influence (ally) - 2 delegates  
            - Winchester: 336 influence (ally) - 2 delegates
            - Zanzibar: 667 influence (ally) - 2 delegates
            - Sofia: defeated (Player 30)
            - That's 8 delegates from city-states
            
            Wait, I need to recount delegates. The report says I have 29 delegates currently.
            
            Other players' stats:
            - Iroquois: 33 delegates, 377k military strength, I'm their vassal (capitulated), they have atomic bombs
            - Brazil: 26 delegates, 8364 gold, +541 GPT, active diplomatic competition
            - Poland: 16 delegates, friendly with me, Autocracy like me
            - Aztecs: 10 delegates, at war with me
            - Shoshone: 6 delegates, at war with me
            
            Active Wars:
            - I'm at war with Aztecs and Shoshone (public -100 with both)
            - I see I am also listed as having denounced Shoshone and Aztecs
            
            Diplomatic Victory Proposal:
            - "Global Hegemony" is up for vote in 10 turns
            - This is the critical vote!
            
            Key insight: I'm currently a vassal of the Iroquois (capitulated). The Iroquois have 33 delegates and 377k military vs my 6.8k military. They also have atomic bombs. I cannot win militarily against them under any circumstances.
            
            The Iroquois also have World Ideology: Autocracy enacted, which is helpful since I'm Autocracy too.
            
            Current Flavors from last turn:
            - UnitedNations grand strategy
            - Very high Defense (95), CityDefense (90), Nuke (90), UseNuke (85), Antiair (85)
            - High Gold (90), Diplomacy (100), Religion (100)
            - Low Offense (20), Expansion (10), Wonder (20)
            
            Research: Next was Electronics (to unlock Police Stations)
            Policy: Next was Enlightenment (Rationalism continuation - free tech)
            
            New situation analysis:
            1. Diplomatic victory vote in 10 turns - this is THE critical window
            2. I have 29 delegates, need 41
            3. My city-state allies: Prague, Wittenberg, Winchester, Zanzibar = 8 delegates
            4. I also get delegates from being host, world religion, world ideology, etc.
            5. I need to secure more votes somehow - either by gaining more city-state allies or by getting other civs to vote for me
            6. Belgrade is at 434 influence (friends, not ally) - could become an ally
            7. Several city-states have quests I can complete
            
            Wait, looking at the Belgrade situation:
            - Belgrade MajorAlly is Iroquois with 660 influence
            - I have 434 influence with Belgrade
            - If I can surpass 660, I'd gain another 2 delegates. This would put me at 31 delegates from city-states plus whatever base delegates I have.
            
            But wait - I need to check if my delegate count already includes base delegates. Yes, my total is 29 delegates currently.
            
            Let me think about what actions to take:
            
            1. Research: Currently on Advanced Ballistics (3 turns left), next was Electronics. Electronics unlocks Police Stations. But with only 10 turns until voting, should I change this? Actually, Electronics → Telecommunications → maybe something else. But I don't think I can complete a whole tech tree before the vote. Keep Electronics.
            
            2. Policy: Enlightenment gives a free tech and +1 happiness from universities. Available in ~8 turns. This is good. Keep it.
            
            3. Flavors: Need to focus HARD on diplomacy and city-state influence. The vote is in 10 turns.
               - Diplomacy: 100 (already maxed effectively)
               - Gold: 90 (need gold to buy influence/buy diplomats)
               - Religion: 100 (city-state quests involve religion)
               
            Should I adjust flavors? I want to prioritize Diplomacy and maybe Mobilization or Gold to generate more diplomatic units or gold to gift city-states.
            
            4. Relationships:
               - Brazil: Used Great Diplomat against me on Zanzibar. They have 26 delegates and are actively competing. -50/-60 relationship. Could adjust this but we're in competition.
               - Iroquois: 33 delegates, 377k military, I'm their vassal, -100/-100. Nothing I can do diplomatically with them.
               - Poland: 16 delegates, friendly, both Autocracy. Could they vote for me? I should make sure they like me a lot.
               
            5. New Relationships needed:
               - Belgrade: Currently friends (434 influence), Iroquois are allies (660). If I can flip Belgrade, that's +2 delegates. This is HUGE.
               
            Wait, I should look at whether I have a spy in Belgrade. Yes: Agent Raden is "Traveling" to Belgrade. Agent Prapana is a diplomat in Rio de Janeiro (Brazil). Agent Indra is a diplomat in Krakow (Poland). 
            
            Agent Raden (Spy) is traveling to Belgrade. If they arrive and can stage a coup, that could flip Belgrade! But traveling takes time.
            
            Actually, looking at Espionage section:
            - Agent Raden (0): State: Traveling, Role: Spy, Location: Belgrade (City-State Belgrade)
            - So a spy is traveling to Belgrade. Good.
            
            But for flavors and strategy, the key is:
            - World Congress in 10 turns
            - I need 12 more delegates to win outright (41 needed, have 29)
            - Or I need to be one of the top 2 vote-getters to gain 5 permanent delegates if no one wins
            - Actually, winning outright requires 41 votes. If no one gets 41, the top 2 get +5 permanent delegates each.
            
            So my strategy should be:
            1. Try to reach 41 delegates in 10 turns - extremely difficult
            2. If not, ensure I'm in the top 2 vote-getters to get +5 delegates for next time
            3. The Iroquois have 33, I have 29, Brazil has 26. I need to beat Brazil at minimum.
            
            Ways to get more delegates:
            - Flip Belgrade: +2 delegates
            - Complete city-state quests for more influence
            - Get more city-state allies (but most are defeated or allied to others)
            - Thematically, I could increase my voting power through wonders or policies but there's no time
            
            Actually I should check if there are other city-states alive:
            Living city-states:
            - Prague (ally)
            - Belgrade (Iroquois ally)
            - Raqmu (Brazil ally, 885 influence)
            - Cape Town (Brazil ally, 942 influence)
            - Zanzibar (my ally)
            - Wittenberg (my ally)
            - Winchester (my ally)
            - Sokoto (Brazil ally, 867 influence)
            
            So only 8 city-states total remain alive. Brazil has 3 allies (Raqmu, Cape Town, Sokoto = 6 delegates), I have 4 allies (Prague, Zanzibar, Wittenberg, Winchester = 8 delegates), Iroquois have 1 ally (Belgrade = 2 delegates).
            
            Wait, but Brazil only has 26 delegates total, I have 29. If Brazil gets 6 from city-states and I get 8 from city-states, that means Brazil has 20 base delegates and I have 21 base delegates. The Iroquois have 33 total, so if they have 2 from city-states, they have 31 base delegates. Poland has 16 = maybe 10 base + some? Aztecs 10, Shoshone 6.
            
            Actually delegate counts in Vox Populi come from: base + host + world religion + world ideology + city-state allies + other factors.
            
            Let me reconsider the strategy. I need about 12 more delegates. The only city-state I can realistically flip is Belgrade. That gives +2 but costs the Iroquois -2, net +4 swing. But I'd still need 8 more.
            
            Looking at this realistically, I probably cannot reach 41 delegates in 10 turns. But I NEED to be in the top 2. The Iroquois (33) and I (29) are currently top 2, with Brazil (26) behind. If Brazil passes me, I drop out of the top 2 and lose the +5 bonus.
            
            Brazil just tried to steal Zanzibar from me and succeeded temporarily. They have massive gold (8364, +541 GPT). They can buy Great Diplomats or gift gold to city-states.
            
            Wait - looking at Brazil's vote count: 26 delegates. But they have 3 city-state allies (6 delegates). So their base is 20. If they somehow flip one of my city-states, they'd gain 2 and I'd lose 2, making it 28 vs 31. That would be dangerous.
            
            I must hold all 4 of my city-state allies at all costs.
            
            Wittenberg has a Hinduism quest with 28 turns remaining. Prague also has a Hinduism quest with 28 turns. I need to ensure Hinduism is spread to these cities.
            
            Zanzibar just flipped back to me. Winchester has Great Artist quest (41 turns).
            
            Wait, I need to check if the quests I'm seeing are still current. In the Players section:
            - Prague quest: Hinduism majority, 28 turns remaining
            - Wittenberg: Great Musician, 16 turns remaining
            - Winchester: Great Artist, 41 turns remaining
            
            Wait, earlier it said Wittenberg Hinduism quest had 4 turns remaining. Now it says Great Musician with 16 turns. That suggests the Hinduism quest may have been completed or timed out.
            
            OK, new events changed things.
            
            Since I have only 10 turns until the vote and realistically need to defend my position, my strategy should be:
            
            1. MAXIMUM DIPLOMACY AND GOLD focus. I need to generate gold to:
               - Buy diplomatic units (Ambassadors, Great Diplomats if possible)
               - Gift to city-states to maintain alliances
               - Complete quests
            
            2. Belgrade is the key target. If I can flip it, total swing is 4 delegates. My spy is traveling there. I should support this however possible, but I can't directly control the spy.
            
            3. I am currently producing Ambassadors in Medan and Makassar (production turns left: 1 each based on the cities report? Actually checking... "ProductionTurnsLeft: 1" for both). So I'll have new ambassadors soon.
            
            4. Persona: High MinorCivFriendlyBias and MinorCivCompetitiveness already set to 10. This is good.
            
            5. For flavors:
               - Diplomacy: 100 (already set)
               - Gold: 100 (increase slightly, need more GPT to buy influence)
               - Religion: 100 (spread Hinduism to complete quests)
               - Science: maybe reduce? Not critical in next 10 turns
               - Spaceship: reduce
               - Defense: keep high because of wars with Aztecs/Shoshone
               - Offense: keep low (20)
            
            Actually, looking at the active wars:
            - Shoshone: 3016 military, they're close but not directly assaulting me heavily based on zones
            - Aztecs: 3031 military, also close
            - My military: 21958 strength, so I outnumber both significantly
            - But I'm at war with both
            
            My military is actually stronger than Aztecs and Shoshone combined (21958 vs ~6000). The Iroquois are the real threat at 377k but they're not at war with me directly.
            
            Given I have military superiority over my active enemies, I could actually try to push for peace or even capture more cities. But capturing cities increases warmongering and could hurt diplomatic standing. Also, Global Peace Accords is active, which increases warmonger penalties.
            
            However, I need to focus on the diplomatic victory. I should probably try to make peace with Aztecs and/or Shoshone to free up resources and reduce war penalties. But my relationship with them is -100 and I set it to -100.
            
            Wait, looking at events, I see:
            - Indonesia made a deal with Aztecs (Event 70): embassy for embassy plus some resources (50 turns). This suggests some diplomatic normalization might be happening despite the war.
            
            Actually, let me reconsider peace. If I make peace with Aztecs, I can focus entirely on defense/diplomacy. But personality settings affect AI willingness to accept peace. My WarBias is 4 (low).
            
            The critical issue is the 10-turn countdown. I cannot meaningfully change my military situation in 10 turns to help diplomatic victory. What I CAN do is:
            1. Ensure I have maximum gold income to purchase diplomatic influence
            2. Ensure diplomatic units are produced and deployed
            3. Try to complete any city-state quests
            4. Possibly adjust my relationship with Poland to get them to vote for me? Actually, allies don't vote for you in Vox Populi unless they're your vassal or you have specific agreements. In World Congress, you vote for yourself ideally. But for "Global Hegemony", others can support you if they choose.
            
            Hmm, actually in the Diplomatic Victory proposal, other civs decide whether to support you. You can't directly control their votes except through making them your vassal or having extremely good relations.
            
            Wait, I just realized - I'm at war with Aztecs and Shoshone, and we're all vassals of the Iroquois. The Iroquois control most of the world.
            
            Given the extreme difficulty of reaching 41 votes, and that the Iroquois (33) and I (29) are currently top 2, my primary goal should be:
            1. Don't let Brazil (26) pass me
            2. Try to gain enough delegates to win outright
            3. Prepare for next vote if this one fails
            
            To not let Brazil pass me, I must hold my 4 city-state allies. Brazil has already demonstrated they will use Great Diplomats against me. They have three city-state allies and massive gold.
            
            My available options:
            
            1. Set Flavors: Increase Gold to 100 (from 90), keep Diplomacy at 100, maybe reduce some military flavors and increase Production to build things faster? Actually Production helps build diplomatic units too. Currently Production is 75. Maybe increase Mobilization to speed up unit production? But civilian buildings are more important.
            
            Actually, looking at available Flavors options, I don't see specific ones for diplomatic units. But "Diplomacy" flavor should prioritize diplomatic unit production.
            
            2. Set Research: Keep Electronics (already set)
            
            3. Set Policy: Keep Enlightenment (already set)
            
            4. Set Relationship with Brazil: Currently -50/-60. They're aggressively competing. Maybe I should actually INCREASE hostility to ensure AI keeps fighting back diplomatically? Or maybe reducing hostility slightly could help? No, Brazil is actively trying to flip my city-states. They used a Great Diplomat on Zanzibar. They'll do it again. Keeping negative relationship sends appropriate signals to AI.
            
            Wait, but looking at the events, Brazil was initially the ally of Zanzibar, then I took it with a Great Diplomat. Then in Turn 424 they flipped it back with a Great Diplomat. Then in Turn 425 I flipped it back. So it's been going back and forth.
            
            Given Brazil's 8364 gold and +541 GPT, they will continue buying influence. I need to prepare for this.
            
            Actually, looking at the relationship between me and Brazil, we have:
            - Declaration of Friendship
            - Open Borders
            - They're my vassal's master (Iroquois)'s other vassal
            - Trade deals active
            
            Wait, I have DoF with Brazil but relationship is -50/-60? That's odd but possible in Civ V when there are conflicting modifiers. We have DoF but I'm also competing with them diplomatically and denounced them? No, I haven't denounced Brazil. But they stole Zanzibar and we have opposing interests.
            
            Should I denounce Brazil? That might not help. 
            
            Actually, looking at my diplomatic persona, I set Friendliness to 9 and WorkWithWillingness to 9. But with Brazil I have -50.
            
            Let me think about whether I need to change anything:
            
            Current approach seems solid. The key adjustments I should consider:
            
            1. Belgrade: I need to reclaim Belgrade if possible. My spy is traveling there. If a coup succeeds, great. But I should also consider sending a Great Diplomat to Belgrade if I get one. Currently my diplomats are deployed elsewhere.
            
            2. If I can't flip Belgrade, I need to ensure Brazil doesn't flip any of mine.
            
            3. Gold generation: My GPT is -296! That's terrible. I'm hemorrhaging gold. I need to fix my economy urgently or I won't be able to buy influence when needed.
            Wait, looking at my stats:
            - Gold: 411
            - GPT: -296
            
            This is bad. I'm losing almost 300 gold per turn. At this rate I'll go broke soon. But I have trade routes generating gold.
            
            Outgoing trade routes show a lot of gold from most routes. For example:
            - Jakarta → Tlatelolco: 48.57 gold
            - Jakarta → Onondaga: 72.76 gold
            Many routes bring in 40-80 gold each, but I'm paying for them too.
            
            Wait, actually the outgoing trade route numbers:
            - FromGold is what I receive, ToGold is what they receive.
            So Jakarta → Onondaga brings me 72.76 gold and costs me nothing? No, wait, "ToGold: 8.75" means the other city gets 8.75, "FromGold: 72.76" means I get 72.76.
            
            But then why is my GPT -296? Let me check city details:
            - Jakarta: GPT 1008
            - Medan: GPT 321
            - Makassar: GPT 212
            - Batam: GPT 207
            - Tlatelolco: GPT 216
            Total from 5 cities: ~1964 gold per turn
            
            But my overall GPT is -296. That means my unit maintenance and building maintenance must be extremely high. Also I have deals where I pay gold to Brazil and Poland and Aztecs.
            
            My military supply is 68, I have 69 units, many of them advanced (tanks, artillery, bombers, etc). The maintenance is killing me.
            
            This is actually a crisis. With -296 GPT, I only have about 1.4 turns of gold left at current spending, but since I get gold per turn, I actually have 411 / 296 = 1.4 turns before bankruptcy. Wait, no - if GPT is net -296, I lose 296 per turn. Starting with 411, I'll hit 0 in less than 2 turns.
            
            Actually, the Golden Age just triggered (Event 78: PlayerGoldenAge, +10 turns). That might help. But bankruptcy means unit disbanding and other penalties. I MUST fix my economy IMMEDIATELY.
            
            How to fix economy:
            - Reduce military unit count (disband old units)
            - Increase Gold flavor to prioritize gold-generating buildings/tiles
            - Peace with enemies reduces war maintenance but Global Peace Accords already increases it by 25%
            - Complete trade route infrastructure
            
            Wait, diplomatic units cost maintenance too. I have a lot of units.
            
            Given the critical gold shortage, I should prioritize Gold flavor even higher, maybe reduce Mobilization or some military flavors. But my cities still need to produce diplomatic units.
            
            Actually, looking at the available options, I don't see flavors for economy vs units explicitly. But I can set:
            - Gold: 100 (maximize gold)
            - Production: maybe increase to build economic buildings?
            - Diplomacy: 100 (diplomatic units are critical)
            
            But I also need to consider that I have units being built:
            - Medan: Ambassador, 1 turn left
            - Makassar: Ambassador, 1 turn left
            
            These will be ready next turn.
            
            My biggest worry is gold. If I run out of gold, I can't upgrade units, maintain my army, or purchase diplomatic influence. Bankruptcy would be catastrophic.
            
            Wait, the report says "Gold: 411" and "GoldPerTurn: -296". But in deals I have:
            - Brazil pays me 7 GPT for Nutmeg
            - I pay Brazil 7 GPT (what? for 1 gold?)
            - Brazil pays me 80 GPT for 4 Coal
            - Brazil pays me 7 GPT for Pepper
            - Brazil pays me 7 GPT for Cloves
            Net from Brazil: ~80 GPT positive
            
            Poland deals:
